1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on materials structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 670. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 262 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the materials space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 670 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 262. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in materials theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 289 and the invariant I(A,B) = 771 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 289 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the materials structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 771. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
